</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Continue after a provider's progress message">

An agent can continue from a progress message to tools and a final answer when a Responses provider explicitly says the turn is unfinished. You no longer need to send another prompt in that case, and cancellation and stop controls still apply.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Avoid compression-related failures in compatible Responses streams [#141592](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/141592). Thanks @anyech, @patrick-erichsen.
- Continue HTTP conversations whose keys contain uppercase letters [#142746](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/142746). Thanks @ruel225, @obviyus, @kevinammerman.
- Propagate cancellation through affected Bun HTTP response streams [#145598](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145598).
- Deliver Bun HTTP rejections without premature resets [#145736](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145736).
- Continue agent work after Responses progress messages that explicitly request another turn [#146393](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146393). Thanks @vacinc.

**Improvements**

- Reduce copying when preparing Anthropic tool definitions [#145287](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145287).
- Reduce repeated processing of streamed HTTP replies [#145683](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145683).
- Skip unnecessary large-number scanning for ordinary text tool arguments [#146241](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146241).
- Reduce local processing of long streamed code replies [#146254](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146254).
- Skip unnecessary JSON reconstruction for streamed ordinary text and Unicode while preserving escape handling [#147101](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147101).
- Reuse tool-definition processing across OpenAI Responses and managed Completions requests while preserving schema changes and separate request payloads [#147543](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147543).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Keep image and PDF requests on the intended model">

Image descriptions and PDF analysis keep the model selected for that request, and PDF results name the selected model consistently. Heavy image and [PDF processing](/tools/pdf) can run separately so other OpenClaw requests continue and cancellation can take effect. Busy workers can reject new work, and their three-minute deadline includes time spent waiting.

Restored Codex screenshots now stay beside their original messages instead of appearing attached to a later request. If a whole historical message no longer fits in the model's context, its images are left out of that request but remain saved.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Use each image-description request's selected model [#144254](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144254).
- Keep PDF model selection and result reporting consistent [#144417](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144417).
- Honor `--agent` in CLI image describe and describe-many, using the selected agent's model and workspace context in multi-agent setups [#147456](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147456). Thanks @obviyus, @mertbasar0.
- Keep image-analysis model aliases and resize limits tied to the supplied runtime through overrides and fallback requests [#147830](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147830).
- Keep restored Codex images with their original historical messages, preserving saved attachments when context is trimmed [#147913](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147913).

**Improvements**

- Decode fewer bytes when identifying supported Anthropic images [#145277](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145277).
- Move image and PDF processing into cancellable workers with overload errors and a three-minute queue-and-execution deadline [#146094](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146094).
- Skip unused structured payload processing when tool results already provide text [#146328](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146328).
- Reduce local processing for large unwrapped image results [#146506](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146506).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Preserve long voice notes and avoid wasted synthesis">

Deepgram Flux preserves voice-note audio beyond 20 minutes instead of silently dropping the end, within your configured size and timeout limits. The speech CLI also rejects an unsupported remote Gateway `--output` request before synthesis starts, avoiding provider usage for a file it cannot save locally. Audio transcription commands now identify the provider and model used when that information is available.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Keep speech summaries on the selected model [#144354](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144354).
- Preserve Deepgram Flux voice-note audio beyond 20 minutes [#145391](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145391). Thanks @obviyus.
- Restore bundled speech-summary models at startup [#146183](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146183).
- Reject unsupported remote speech downloads before synthesis starts [#146222](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146222). Thanks @leon-sk668.
- Resolve Kimi speech-summary aliases before provider startup [#146261](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146261).
- Show returned provider and model attribution in audio transcription command output, including JSON and the capability alias [#147170](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147170).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Connect organization models through Radius">

The new [Radius provider](/providers/radius) connects your organization's model access through browser sign-in or an API key, with streamed answers and supported image and tool use. Requests use the selected organization's credits and follow its budgets and policies. Available models depend on that access, and some advertised models can still be unavailable upstream.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Add Radius organization models with browser sign-in and native streaming [#145377](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145377).

**Bug fixes**

- Send valid Radius Claude requests with thinking disabled [#145565](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145565).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="See why a search failed">

When every automatic web-search provider fails, operator logs retain the first failure to help you trace the problem. The docs CLI also reports malformed search responses as errors, so a broken response no longer looks like a successful search with no matches. Known HTTP errors return without waiting for diagnostic response capture to finish.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Retain the first error when all automatic search providers fail [#132755](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/132755). Thanks @tommyjoseph, @obviyus.
- Report malformed docs search responses as errors [#145613](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145613).
- Report known docs-search HTTP errors promptly, allowing failed-response diagnostic bodies to be partial or empty [#146987](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146987). Thanks @alix-007, @obviyus.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Automatic Daybreak retries">

Eligible defensive-security requests can receive one automatic Daybreak retry after a structured OpenAI policy refusal, in both native Codex and embedded OpenAI chats. The retry keeps your selected session model in place. Successful interactive embedded retries name Daybreak or your configured retry model before the answer, including in the originating group or channel.

Automatic retries are enabled for eligible unlocked settings and require access granted by the provider. An unavailable target can add a request and delay. Strict model selections, other refusal types and work that cannot safely be repeated still stop without a retry. To disable retries, set `plugins.entries.codex.config.appServer.cyberFailover.mode` to `off` for native Codex, or `agents.defaults.embeddedAgent.cyberFailover.mode` to `off` for embedded turns. See [model failover](/concepts/model-failover) for configuration.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Show Codex policy notices and add a conditional, single-turn Daybreak retry [#143761](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/143761).
- Extend eligible OpenAI cyber-policy retries to embedded turns with replay checks and preservation of completed work and interruptions [#145291](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145291). Thanks @vacinc.
- Name the successful embedded retry model before the answer in the originating conversation [#147146](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147146). Thanks @vacinc.

**Bug fixes**

- Handle denied Codex fallback access with an unavailable notice and cooldown while preserving attempt results [#145105](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145105).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Keep generated media filenames">

Generated music attachments now use the saved audio filename, including a name you supplied, so immediate results and background completion messages identify the same file. Generated images and saved videos also keep readable filenames in their results and completion messages while retaining their unique storage paths. Videos returned only as links keep their existing names.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Keep the saved, sanitized audio filename in generated music results and background completion messages [#147570](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147570).
- Use readable saved filenames for generated image and video attachments and completion text [#147833](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147833).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Choose the same model through different runtimes">

You can offer the same model through OpenClaw and Codex as separate, labeled choices in the chat model picker. Each choice shows the reasoning and context options that its runtime supports. To enable this, set `pickerRuntimes` on an exact model entry, with up to eight explicit runtime IDs. Your configured `agentRuntime` stays the default. Per-agent lists replace inherited alternatives, and an empty list removes them. See the [models guide](/concepts/models) for configuration.

Each alternative needs an enabled, compatible runtime and current account access. Adding a picker choice grants no credentials or access. ACP and locked sessions keep their runtime restrictions, and unavailable saved alternatives are discarded rather than silently replaced. Use explicit runtime IDs rather than `auto` or `default`; wildcard model entries are excluded. Opening the picker uses prepared choices, while Refresh checks for new ones.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Offer opt-in runtime alternatives for the same model with runtime-specific capabilities, account checks and preserved default routing [#147994](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147994).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Reduce retained text during long replies">

During long ordinary-text replies through Completions, OpenClaw keeps less accumulated text in memory as the answer arrives. Completions and Responses also avoid repeated waits when a scheduled pause runs longer than expected. The reply text and cancellation checks stay intact. Processing time varies, and short replies can take longer.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Reduce accumulated source-text retention in ordinary Completions replies and redundant scheduling waits in Completions and Responses [#147972](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147972).

**Limits and compatibility**

- Buffered parsing paths remain outside the ordinary-text retention repair, and a single expensive event cannot be interrupted. The retention repair can take more processing time than the scheduling change alone; measured string storage is not total process memory.

</details>

</Accordion>

</AccordionGroup>

## Automations and Scheduling

Scheduled work keeps your latest edits and gives you clearer ways to follow what ran, what failed, and what needs attention. You can also see whether delegated work is running, waiting or finished, and whether its result has returned.

<AccordionGroup>

<Accordion title="Keep scheduled work and its outcomes current">

Changes you save to a running schedule or watcher now survive completion and crash recovery. The CLI also makes it easier to investigate a run, with searchable, paged history and separate filters for execution and delivery results.

After a plugin reload, script automations can refresh stale tools once before starting. Repeated failures stay grouped, and confirmed successful completion sends a recovery notice. Setup recovery never replays a script or tool action that has already started.

Doctor now preserves scheduled reports that return to their original conversation, including setups without an external messaging channel. If an older repair already changed a job to isolated delivery, restore its intended destination manually. Disabled jobs stay disabled.

Before downgrading, let affected edited runs finish and let the scheduler finish reconciling their results on the current version. A completed task alone is not enough to preserve those edits when returning to an older version.

Administrator tasks can keep [managing existing automations](/automation/cron-jobs/managing-jobs) after waiting for subagents, without another user message. That access belongs only to the exact resumed task and covers management of existing jobs. Cancellation, a session reset or archive, a new direct user turn, or a Gateway restart ends the handoff.

Click **Automation attached** in a session hovercard to open its automation editor. If several automations match, you get a list filtered to that session, with **Show all automations** to return to the full list. Your existing viewing and editing permissions still apply.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Show readable next-wake times in cron debug logs [#141742](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/141742). Thanks @alix-007 and @celthi.
- Search, filter and page automation run history from the CLI [#141777](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/141777). Thanks @alix-007 and @patrick-erichsen.
- Reduce repeated filesystem scans in cron delivery previews [#144145](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144145).
- Reduce unnecessary startup work for heartbeat and reply checks [#145033](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145033).
- Avoid repeated message-list copies when filtering heartbeat alerts [#146589](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146589).
- Reduce repeated wake-scheduling work during notice bursts across many sessions [#146818](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146818).
- Reduce heartbeat setup and cron-planning work for large agent fleets without changing frequency or delivery policy [#147055](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147055).
- Avoid repeated database reads when matching standing reminders [#147099](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147099).
- Use fewer database calls when many standing reminders finish their cooldown together [#147180](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147180).
- Reduce repeated processing when listing the automations visible to an agent [#147558](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147558).
- Avoid rereading old prompts and descriptions during ordinary scheduled-job saves [#147743](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147743).
- Open attached automations directly from a session hovercard, with a filtered list for multiple matches [#147749](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147749).

**Bug fixes**

- Reduce cron cleanup discovery stalls across large agent fleets [#142591](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/142591). Thanks @zachisfine, @pollybot13, and @609nft.
- Forward saved CLI credentials to scheduled and queued runs [#144266](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144266). Thanks @masterswords1, @fuller-stack-dev, @angeliti999, and @iwhatsskill.
- Preserve heartbeat alerts and private notes through retries [#144377](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144377). Thanks @obviyus and @jlahullier.
- Accept zero scheduler timeouts when adding or editing automations, while provider, startup and command-runner limits still apply [#144551](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144551). Thanks @jalehman.
- Preserve standing reminders after prompt-hook timeouts [#145107](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145107).
- Release old Automation page state after changing agents [#145236](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145236).
- Preserve running automation edits through completion and crash recovery [#145375](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145375). Thanks @shakkernerd.
- Preserve spaces in quoted automation script filenames [#145673](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145673).
- Recover the correct cron run and preserve watcher edits [#145734](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145734). Thanks @shakkernerd.
- Preserve edited cron schedules during recovery [#145828](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145828). Thanks @shakkernerd.
- Preserve cron failure details when an active session is renamed [#146551](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146551). Thanks @vacinc.
- Recover automation setup after plugin reloads and group ongoing failure alerts [#146582](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146582).
- Show active automation runs as running even when their stream source is disabled [#146793](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146793).
- Preserve current-conversation delivery through Doctor repair [#146794](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146794). Thanks @fuller-stack-dev.
- Honor permitted quiet heartbeat results without an unwanted missing-summary warning or another finalizer request [#145343](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145343). Thanks @pollybot13, @nixcyry, and @obviyus.
- Keep run history with the selected automation when another task's Run now request finishes late [#147399](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147399).
- Let resumed administrator tasks manage existing automations after waiting for subagents, without granting creation rights or changing scheduled execution policy [#147499](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147499).
- Keep automation fields focused as validation messages appear or clear, so typing can continue [#147841](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147841).
- Resolve heartbeat owners through operation-scoped channels and show the running Gateway's readiness in connected CLI status [#147944](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147944). Thanks @perpaul.
- Reject blank CLI schedule values before creating or updating jobs, while preserving metadata edits that omit schedule flags [#148271](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/148271).

**Limits and compatibility**

- Offline status and failed Gateway status calls retain the local readiness fallback. [Heartbeat setup](/gateway/heartbeat) uses an array for `commands.ownerAllowFrom`; preserve existing owners when replacing it. For Telegram, set `heartbeat.target` to `"telegram"` and `heartbeat.to` separately to a quoted recipient string.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Automation owners and maintenance notices">

If Doctor can identify who created an older automation, `openclaw doctor --fix` restores their ability to edit its prompt without changing tool permissions. Jobs now stop before execution and explain when a required creator account is unavailable. Restore that account or recreate the automation under the intended account. Changing its delivery account does not transfer ownership, and a missing execution agent leaves a reason in run history.

Agents are now instructed to ask an authorized administrator to check a known automation when a restricted lookup cannot find it. The administrator can use the Automations page or a fresh authenticated Control UI administrator turn. A missing-job response alone is not a reason to create a replacement.

Doctor also warns when some automatically captured tool lists may be missing file, command or web capabilities. The restrictions may be intentional, so even `--fix` leaves them unchanged. To add tools, an authorized session must review and replace the complete list, including every tool the job should keep.

New update and restart notices go only to destinations matched to configured command owners, and update notices respect account send settings. Unsupported destinations lose notices and restart continuations, while update results remain in the Control UI. Review owner eligibility and account send settings if chat notices stop arriving. Later policy changes do not recall notices already queued.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Record missing-owner rejections for manual automation runs [#143804](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/143804). Thanks @sunnyshu0925, @obviyus, and @yncubys.
- Restore creator prompt editing for older automations [#145730](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145730). Thanks @joncursi.
- Report unavailable-account and missing-agent automation failures [#145783](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145783). Thanks @runvouch and @joncursi.
- Guide agents to seek an authorized administrator's check before replacing a known automation after a restricted lookup [#137451](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/137451). Thanks @liuwqgit, @sallyom, @eason-mars, @pkinjo, and @emmyallears.

**Improvements**

- Warn about potentially incomplete automatically captured tool lists and explain authorized complete-list review without widening permissions [#147969](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147969). Thanks @obviyus.

**Security and trust**

- Restrict lifecycle notices to configured owners [#146079](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146079). Thanks @adembektas.
- Respect account send settings for update notices [#146084](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146084). Thanks @eleqtrizit and @adembektas.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Finish automatic reset hooks">

Hooks that run during daily or idle session resets can now finish delayed work after the triggering request ends. This lets a hook wait for another operation and continue its work without losing its place when that request finishes.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Let delayed automatic reset hooks finish after the triggering request ends [#146350](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146350). Thanks @vasubansal7576, @liuwqgit, @vincentkoc, and @yigtwxx.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Review a subagent's result before announcing it">

A parent agent can now [review a subagent's result privately](/tools/subagents/announce#private-parent-completion), ask for more work, or stay silent before anything is automatically announced to a channel. Opt in with `completionTarget: "parent"` when spawning the subagent. A busy parent handles the result in a separate private turn after its current work, and resetting or removing the parent keeps the result from reaching a replacement session.

This option supports hidden native one-shot runs. ACP, collected results, visible or threaded runs, session mode, and `expectsCompletionMessage: false` are not supported. Automatic child results, parent replies and generated media stay off the channel on this route, but permitted explicit send tools can still send messages, and operators can still inspect tool arguments and child transcripts. Leaving the option unset preserves existing behavior.

Keep a verified pre-upgrade backup and follow the target build's migration instructions, including stopping processes that write to OpenClaw's data before running `doctor --fix`. Rolling back requires the matching released build and backup, losing work saved after that backup. Older builds may discard private handoffs, so downgrading and upgrading again does not guarantee they will resume.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Return native subagent results for private parent review, follow-up work or silence before automatic channel announcements [#147206](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147206). Thanks @vincentkoc, @nicknmorty, @good-warning, and @fuller-stack-dev.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Delegate work to a selected project">

An agent can now open a separately visible child session for a [registered project](/tools/subagents/tool-reference#param-project-id) or [managed GitHub repository](/tools/subagents/tool-reference#param-project-git-url), optionally in its own worktree. This gives delegated work its own place to run and a session you can follow.

Use a visible native subagent with exactly one of `projectId` or `projectGitUrl`, leaving `cwd` unset. GitHub HTTPS and `git@github.com` repository URLs are supported; local paths, file URLs, other Git hosts, hidden runs and ACP runs are excluded. Existing sandbox boundaries, tool limits and setup-script permissions still apply, and arbitrary external directories still require administrator access.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Select a registered project or managed GitHub repository for a visible child session, with optional worktree creation [#147459](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147459). Thanks @vacinc and @yasuhito.

**Limits and compatibility**

- With `worktree: true` and no project or directory selector, same-agent children continue to inherit the parent repository.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Follow delegated work and wait for results">

The [task inspector](/automation/tasks) now shows whether a subagent is working, waiting or finished, and whether its result has reached the parent. You can inspect its activity and child dependencies beside the parent conversation without losing your draft. When a runtime cannot report current activity, the inspector leaves it unknown.

Agents can wait for selected tasks to finish or need attention. Explicit message modes let them supply future context, steer supported active work, or start a follow-up turn. Ending a wait or reaching its time limit leaves the tasks running and their results available for delivery. The `notify` mode queues context for a future turn without waking the target or confirming it has read the message. That queued context can be evicted or lost on restart.

Scheduled jobs wait for delegated work and its result delivery before choosing a final response, within their existing deadline. Repeated wait checks now leave time to process completion. Managed [Lobster approval flows](/tools/lobster) can also resume from a saved checkpoint with the flow ID, current revision, owning session and the user's decision.

[Background commands](/gateway/background-process) still do not automatically wake a subagent. Collect their terminal results with `process poll` before yielding without another completion source, including after a requested stop. Confirmed requested stops with successful cleanup avoid false failure notices, while real timeouts and cleanup failures remain errors. A CI watcher that exits with `PROXY-AUTH-FAILED` and code 2 needs a new watcher from an active authenticated run or repaired proxy access.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Show current subagent activity, waits and result delivery, with selected-task waits, explicit message modes, optional progress notices and saved Lobster checkpoint recovery [#147571](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147571).

**Bug fixes**

- Return the recorded answer, completion receipt and accurate timeout or cancellation outcome when chat delivery finishes [#147656](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147656). Thanks @vincentkoc.
- Leave time for completion callbacks when scheduled jobs repeatedly receive immediate subagent wait responses [#147779](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147779).
- Preserve requested-stop outcomes, require collection of background-command results before unsupported yields, and stop CI watchers on expired proxy access [#147871](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147871).

**Limits and compatibility**

- Selected-task waits accept 1–32 task IDs for 0–60 seconds and distinguish completion, attention, unavailability and timeout. `steer` requires a supported active run; `followup` starts or queues another turn.
- Yielding without children requires explicit `waitFor: "message"` and a real future sender. A waiting status does not schedule an external timer or guarantee a future wakeup.
- Post-yield `state_changes` progress is opt-in, bounded and best effort. It is process-local and excludes private child text, tool arguments and results.
- Workboard claims coordinate cooperating writers but do not protect files or shell processes from other writers. Use separate worktrees for isolation.

</details>

</Accordion>

</AccordionGroup>

## Browser and Computer Use

You can share a browser page with your agent and use computer controls on supported local or cloud desktops. Cloud sessions also gain more ways to reuse prepared work and recover after interruptions.

<AccordionGroup>

<Accordion title="Start work from prepared cloud environments">

Private repositories can reuse eligible [prepared cloud environments](/gateway/cloud-workers/warm-images), reducing repeated setup when matching capacity is available. Your source GitHub credentials stay on the computer running OpenClaw during preparation. The connected session receives its normal credentials later.

Crabbox-based workers require version 0.56.0 or newer. If your host cannot download releases, stage a supported copy before updating OpenClaw, because worker inspection and cleanup need it too.

File checks for large workspaces are also faster when preparing node and cloud sessions. Stop can cancel preparation before a worker is allocated, though file reads already underway must finish before cleanup. Upgrade the Gateway to receive this fix; updating only the node is not enough.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Reuse prepared cloud environments for private repositories [#144237](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144237).
- Avoid extra remote calls when starting prepared worker repositories [#145302](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145302).
- Avoid repeated checkpoint cleanup during worker startup [#145331](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145331).
- Reduce GitHub round trips when starting prepared repository workers [#145480](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145480).
- Defer unused syntax highlighting during cloud worker startup [#145685](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145685).
- Overlap cloud worker loading with connection approval [#145793](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145793).
- Skip redundant Git author lookups during worker workspace sync [#146833](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146833). Thanks @vincentkoc.
- Prepare missing worker bundles during remote installation and skip unused archive parsing on cold cloud starts [#147787](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147787).
- Skip unnecessary legacy-state work when activating plugins on fresh cloud workers [#147945](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147945).

**Bug fixes**

- Reject incompatible model switches on worker-assigned conversations [#136833](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/136833). Thanks @sunnyshu0925, @patrick-erichsen, @jalehman, and @skorfmann.
- Avoid loading inactive conversation branches for cloud-worker preparation [#145591](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145591).
- Stop expired forks from extending snapshot demand [#146163](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146163).
- Prevent large-workspace preparation stalls and honor Stop before worker allocation [#147470](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147470).

**Limits and compatibility**

- Require Crabbox 0.56.0 for prepared cloud workers [#145109](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145109).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Resume or recover cloud sessions">

A first message waiting for cloud setup can continue after a Gateway restart. Returning to a released session now waits for its replacement worker without reporting a false stall, and Stop prevents canceled setup from launching work later.

For a failed session, choose **Gateway · local** in **Restart session…** to [recover on the computer running OpenClaw](/gateway/cloud-workers/session-lifecycle) once worker cleanup finishes. Recovery uses the last accepted workspace checkpoint. Changes that were never uploaded may be lost, and the interrupted turn is not replayed.

Archiving an independent cloud session no longer waits for unrelated setup maintenance. Its sidebar row hides immediately and returns with an error if the request is rejected. An already-failed worker with no live turn can stay archived while cleanup is retried separately. **Undo** restores the row while the retained checkout is intact; it does not restart the worker.

Active and provisioning workers still follow their normal steps for stopping and saving work. Deleting or rebuilding a checkout also keeps stricter cleanup checks. Update both the Gateway and Control UI for this behavior, and check the actual archive state after a timeout.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Recover failed cloud sessions locally [#146054](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146054).
- Keep a healthy Crabbox desktop renderer running on resume when its wallpaper is unchanged [#146816](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146816). Thanks @vincentkoc.

**Bug fixes**

- Keep useful failure details for worker workspace transfers [#144272](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144272). Thanks @jalehman.
- Resume cloud-session setup after Gateway restarts [#145068](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145068).
- Show useful cloud worker recovery actions across session menus [#145153](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145153).
- Archive cloud sessions after automatically stopping startup or retrying cleanup [#145170](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145170).
- Resume cloud sessions without false stalls or late worker launches after Stop [#146225](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146225).
- Avoid unrelated queued-maintenance delays and reject duplicate archive requests [#147552](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147552).
- Hide archived sessions immediately and retain failed-worker recovery data while cleanup is pending [#147651](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147651).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Check cloud work with less repeated file processing">

Finishing cloud work can avoid repeatedly reading unchanged files, while changed files still receive the checks needed before their results are accepted. Large workspace scans also reuse temporary memory for file checks.

Large transfers and result checks now run expensive file calculations in the background so other requests can stay responsive. This can use more CPU and memory and does not necessarily shorten the transfer.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Reduce repeated Git work when saving cloud-session checkpoints [#145571](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145571).
- Skip empty provisioned-file archive scans [#146097](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146097).
- Reuse unchanged cloud-workspace file hashes while preserving verification [#146327](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146327).
- Reuse bounded hashing buffers during large cloud-workspace scans [#146680](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146680).
- Run workspace-transfer and result computations in a pool of up to two background workers while keeping writes and cleanup under host control [#147626](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147626).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Manage cloud snapshot build results">

The Snapshots page keeps build progress, cancellation controls, and failure messages visible even when the image list fails to load. Dismiss a reviewed failure and it disappears from the current view, updating **Needs attention** as soon as the request succeeds. The retained record can return after a page reload.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Dismiss reviewed failed cloud-worker snapshot builds from the current view [#144671](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144671).

**Bug fixes**

- Keep snapshot builds visible when image inventory fails [#144127](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144127).
- Remove dismissed snapshot builds immediately [#145122](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145122).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Run sandboxed tools on compatible cloud workers">

The optional [Crabbox sandbox backend](/gateway/sandboxing/crabbox-backend) can run commands and file tools on a cloud machine while your agent and model credentials stay on the host. Enable and configure the plugin to use it. Initial support is direct Daytona with a compatible Crabbox build; version 0.56.0 lacks the required capability, so prepared-worker support alone is not enough. Cloud resource usage applies, and this backend does not support sandboxed browsers or Docker bind mounts.

Before downgrading to a version without this recovery support, disable the backend and finish pending cloud allocation or cleanup using the current version. The shared [SSH backend](/gateway/sandboxing/ssh-backend) also now requires native no-replace directory renaming to create or recreate workspaces. Existing workspaces remain usable without being reseeded.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Run sandboxed tools on compatible Crabbox cloud machines, preserve remote edits across reuse, and publish initial SSH workspace uploads without replacing existing work [#144454](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144454).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Inspect and control the intended desktop">

An agent on the computer running OpenClaw can now [control its configured desktop](/gateway/config-browser-ui-desktop) without a separately paired node. Enable `cua-computer`, allow the `computer` tool, and use a vision-capable agent. The configured Gateway desktop is preferred by default. If it is unavailable, the request fails without sending input elsewhere. Provider setup and native display permissions still apply.

On [desktop-enabled Linux cloud workers](/gateway/cloud-sessions), agents can launch a graphical app with `exec` and use it on the same desktop. Use `background: true` for apps that need to stay open.

The viewer's **Match** option resizes compatible virtual desktops to fit the panel when you have control and permission to resize. **Fit** remains the default, and older fixed-size workers need a backend update and reprovisioning. Supported browsers in a secure context can also open a view-only **Picture-in-Picture** window, letting you watch the desktop beside other work. Keep the originating viewer open.

Supported [window actions](/nodes/computer-use) return a fresh screenshot and element references for the agent's next step. If input or the refresh fails, get a fresh observation before deciding what to do next. Input is not automatically replayed.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Watch live desktops in a view-only Picture-in-Picture window [#143836](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/143836). Thanks @vincentkoc.
- Match compatible virtual desktop resolution to its viewer and clear modifier keys released while native menus were open [#143938](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/143938). Thanks @vincentkoc.
- Reduce host-selection and desktop lookup work [#146162](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146162).
- Avoid repeated screenshot encoding when trimming oversized worker requests [#146907](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146907). Thanks @vincentkoc.
- Control the configured Gateway desktop without pairing a node [#147275](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147275).

**Bug fixes**

- Honor screenshot-free accessibility inspection on updated CUA nodes; Peekaboo remains unsupported and pixel clicks still require an image [#145351](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145351).
- Retain desktop input feedback and explain supported controls [#145352](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145352).
- Restore desktop and portal streaming on Bun [#146005](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146005).
- Keep host availability current during event bursts [#146165](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146165).
- Open graphical apps on the cloud desktop from worker commands [#146223](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146223).
- Clarify supported window accessibility filters for computer-use agents [#146388](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146388).
- Return fresh window images and element references after supported actions [#146822](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146822). Thanks @vincentkoc.
- Retain connected Desktop viewers for quick Chat tab switches within 30 seconds, pausing input while hidden and disconnecting during active drags or session changes [#147175](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147175).
- Release the desktop keyboard's old viewer connection after a full reset without waiting for another input event [#148082](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/148082). Thanks @vincentkoc.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Inspect browser pages and apply policy changes">

Browser pages appear directly in the Chat panel header, leaving more room for the page. Mac Browser panes also stay visible beside menus that do not overlap them. Plain accessibility snapshots show references beside their controls, helping agents target follow-up actions on supported backends.

With [hot reload](/gateway/configuration/hot-reload) enabled, you can change browser enablement, JavaScript permission, and network policy without restarting the whole Gateway. The browser service still restarts, canceling pending work and closing OpenClaw-managed Chrome tabs. Attached and remote browsers stay open, but OpenClaw disconnects from them. Extension relay settings still require a Gateway restart.

If Chrome loses a tab's internal automation target, the [extension relay](/tools/chrome-extension) can restore control for clients that remain subscribed, provided the tab is still accessible. Take a fresh snapshot before continuing; failed actions are not replayed. Explicit detach and Chrome's **Cancel** still stop control.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Show browser pages and optional site icons in the Chat panel header [#145517](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145517).
- Reload browser policy without a Gateway restart [#146100](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146100).
- Skip unnecessary HTML visibility checks [#146152](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146152).
- Format oversized browser request and error logs with less repeated work [#146375](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146375).

**Bug fixes**

- Preserve visible webpage text after hidden HTML [#144210](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144210). Thanks @ruel225, @obviyus, and @yetval.
- Keep sidebar tab menus visible over native Browser pages [#145108](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145108). Thanks @patrick-erichsen.
- Warn when browser response-body output is shortened [#145210](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145210).
- Keep Mac Browser panes visible beside nonoverlapping menus [#145482](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145482).
- Show element references in plain browser accessibility snapshots [#146247](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146247).
- Report unconfirmed browser data removal when deleting a profile, rather than claiming no data exists [#146965](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146965).
- Keep `--browser` in sandbox inspection and recreation advice so follow-up commands target browser containers [#147859](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147859).
- Restore still-subscribed accessible Chrome tabs after native target loss while rejecting commands for retired sessions [#147943](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147943).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Save a displayed browser file">

The [Browser sidebar](/web/control-ui/panels) gains a **Download file** button for the image, video, or document you are viewing, keeping its preview open. Mac tabs use a native Save dialog. Eligible Agent tabs download through your browser; default or network-restricted Agent profiles must use **Open in your browser** instead. Remote browser-node transfers retain their 10 MiB file limit, and existing-session Chrome MCP profiles do not support this action.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Download the displayed Browser sidebar file while keeping its preview open, preserve existing files if a native save fails, and restore missing WebKit toolbar icons [#144480](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144480). Thanks @patrick-erichsen.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Share a browser page with your agent">

A [shared Browser dashboard](/web/dashboards#share-a-browser-dashboard-with-your-agent) lets you and your agent use the same page. Enter a note or change an app's filters, then ask your agent to continue from what you changed. It uses a local OpenClaw-managed browser profile and that profile's login state, including for permitted HTTP apps shown through the HTTPS Control UI.

Browser access is required. Remote profiles, paired-node browsers, and attached personal browsers are unsupported, and the page does not use your phone or laptop's cookies. **Stop** closes the tab and loses unsaved page state. **Resume** opens the saved URL.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Share clicks, typing, and navigation with your agent in one managed-browser dashboard, with saved Stop and Resume controls [#146702](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146702).
- Recheck only selected browser dashboard tabs during cleanup, so unrelated damaged records do not block those checks [#147892](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147892).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Choose which machine handles browser actions">

Automatic browser actions now prefer the selected browser on the computer running OpenClaw, even if an installed managed browser has not started yet. If that computer has no browser capability, a single eligible connected node can handle the request. Explicit targets and configured node pins take precedence. A failed action stays with the selected browser instead of being replayed on another machine.

This default can change which machine and browser login session you use after upgrading. To keep using a node's existing session, [select that node explicitly or set `gateway.nodes.browser.node`](/tools/browser/remote).

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Prefer the Gateway host's selected browser for automatic tool and CLI requests, while preserving explicit node selection [#147446](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147446).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Use real paths for browser output">

Browser downloads and traces preserve the directory name you requested, including trailing spaces, and check the whole path before writing. If your output directory passes through a user-created symbolic link, [choose its real, canonical path](/tools/browser/troubleshooting). The **Invalid path** error can now apply even when the final directory already exists. The macOS `/tmp` and `/var` system aliases remain supported.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Preserve literal browser output directory names, reject symlinked ancestors, and clean up temporary Chrome MCP screenshot workspaces [#147541](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147541).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Start remote tasks in an empty workspace">

Choose **New workspace** to start a task on a [cloud worker](/gateway/cloud-workers/dispatching-a-session) or [eligible paired device](/nodes/session-hosting) without supplying a repository. Remote sessions choose it by default when you have not explicitly selected a folder, project, or worktree. The selection survives draft reload and placement recovery, while explicit source choices keep their existing validation.

Existing agent files are not copied. Each new session starts with its own Git history and remotes, and Git remains an internal requirement. Normal [managed snapshots and recovery](/concepts/managed-worktrees) still apply; this does not add disposable workspaces or general multi-repository support.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Start cloud and eligible paired-device sessions in an isolated empty workspace without a user-supplied repository [#147773](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147773). Thanks @danhayman.

</details>

</Accordion>

</AccordionGroup>

## Plugins and Integrations

Install or reload supported plugins without restarting the Gateway. You can also give teammates read-only access to selected conversations, carry agent settings between installations, and work with the history your integrations save.

<AccordionGroup>

<Accordion title="Install plugins and continue the conversation">

Owners can [install and reload supported plugins](/plugins/manage-plugins) from the CLI or an authorized chat command without restarting a running Gateway. The Plugins UI offers official and ClawHub packages, with existing permission checks and consent still in place. Explicitly approved package activation also works when passive reload is disabled. Managed Codex conversations can continue with changed tools, keeping completed results and accepted follow-up input. This stops the current native turn and background terminals. Chats already accepted but waiting to start can continue after plugin replacement or successful rollback without being resent.

Local paths, archives, local Git repositories and marketplace registrations must be available on the Gateway computer. Offline installs wait for startup. If an installation saves but activation fails, follow its repair message and run `openclaw plugins reload`. An update or repair that loses permission to continue stops and retains installed files and records for a fresh operation. Imported or supervised Codex sessions need a new managed conversation for changed tools. Enabling experimental native plugin UI still requires a Gateway restart and browser reload.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Retain plugin dependencies through active calls and cleanup [#144252](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144252).

- Keep plugin callbacks working when another instance retires [#145804](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145804).
- Recover plugin reloads and retain cleanup for late-starting services [#145816](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145816).
- Add native compiled-plugin loading on Bun, with a remaining Bun 1.4.0 dependency-resolution risk [#145947](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145947).
- Release retired service state after plugin reloads [#146073](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146073). Thanks @vincentkoc.
- Release completed plugin reload state in long-running Gateways [#146239](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146239).
- Apply approved plugin packages through the running Gateway and give accurate reload guidance [#146413](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146413).
- Apply first channel setup through live plugin reload [#146481](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146481).
- Preserve the original plugin update error through rollback failures [#146483](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146483). Thanks @aniruddhaadak80.
- Reuse completed plugin registrations for prepared-metadata loads [#146703](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146703).

- Keep deferred plugin CLI actions and channel setup connected to their initialized runtime [#147688](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147688). Thanks @romneyda.
- Preserve package layout so reloaded plugins can find already-built native addons such as better-sqlite3 [#148170](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/148170). Thanks @novaunboundai. Missing or incompatible binaries still need separate repair.

**Improvements**

- Avoid repeated package discovery during standalone plugin enable and disable operations, preserving fresh discovery for running Gateways and installation changes [#148168](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/148168).

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Find plugins by purpose and inspect them">

Purpose-based categories and package logos make plugins easier to recognize and browse. Marketplace pages keep their destination when you refresh, and a selected detail tab stays open as information arrives. Plugin authors also get clearer build and verification errors when a package is missing its manifest or a declared JavaScript entry. Builds preserve existing manifest and package-folder access permissions.

Plugin settings and installation forms now accept supported credentials such as Brave and Firecrawl keys in masked text fields. Existing structured secret references and redacted values stay concealed and protected; changing a reference still requires editing the configuration file directly.

<Accordion title="Carry agent setup in Claw packages">

Experimental [Claw packages](/cli/claws) now preserve explicit model preferences and delegation settings. With a local Gateway running, add and update can activate up to 64 required plugins together after the usual trust and capability confirmations. Missing models and companion agents still need to be configured separately.

Earlier successful steps can remain after a later failure, so follow the reported recovery instructions before retrying. An interrupted installation can also refuse to resume if model or companion-agent availability changes. Offline installation still needs a restart. Removing a Claw leaves global plugins installed by default; explicitly selected plugin removal waits for active work to finish.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Preserve model preferences and delegation settings in Claw packages [#141921](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/141921).
- Apply Claw plugin requirements together without restarting Gateway [#145713](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145713).

**Security and trust**

- Disclose inherited memory-source additions in Claw update previews [#144415](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144415).

**Bug fixes**

- Drain active plugin work before selected Claw package removal [#145649](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145649).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Share selected conversations read-only">

[Session Share](/plugins/session-share) lets teammates on another paired Gateway read selected conversation groups. Both sides must opt in, and an empty group selection shares nothing. Recipients see conversation text and eligible user-created forks, without subagents, tool activity or reasoning. They can read the conversation but cannot continue it, archive it or open its terminal.

Choose groups carefully, since text and metadata may contain private information and revocation cannot recall what someone already received. Keep the source node connected and restart it after configuration changes. Reload an older open shared view after updating, and restore cold history on the source before reading it remotely.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Security and trust**

- Exclude subagents and tool activity from shared conversation views [#146598](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146598).

**Improvements**

- Share selected native conversations read-only through a restricted paired node [5278a8f2](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/5278a8f2ed9ef49819d483e298d61f3d2656451b).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Browse sessions from installed coding apps">

Choose which installed coding apps supply sidebar conversations in Settings → Appearance → Session sources. Claude Code, Codex, OpenCode and Pi now have one place for these controls. Updated paired nodes can show native Codex conversations without a matching Gateway agent, and empty catalogs stay hidden while discovery continues. Large CLI history imports also preserve more conversation text and visible results when the response needs to fit within its size limit.

These settings autosave for everyone on the Gateway and require a restart; the relevant plugin must be enabled. Update paired nodes as well as the Gateway for the newer Codex discovery behavior. Fresh Claude Code and Codex discovery defaults off, while OpenCode and Pi defaults on. New Claude CLI conversations can use saved subscription tokens while existing conversations retain their account and history. API keys require explicit selection, and your configured fallback policy still applies.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Preserve conversation and visible results in large CLI history imports [#143995](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/143995). Thanks @von8794, @obviyus.
- Preserve Claude CLI answers through backend plugin replacement [#145378](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145378). Thanks @ylcn91, @jalehman, @emi-ercel.
- Keep other Gateway work moving during Claude history discovery [#145684](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145684).
- Use saved subscription tokens in new Claude chats [#146095](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146095). Thanks @obviyus, @klaussarros.
- Restore native Codex session discovery and hide empty sidebar catalogs [#146305](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146305).
- Free session catalog queue capacity when waiting requests are cancelled [#146498](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146498).

**Improvements**

- Reduce repeated work importing long conversations [#146031](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146031).
- Manage coding-app session discovery in one settings panel [#146502](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146502).
- Add coding-app icons to session source settings [#146592](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146592).
- Reduce repeated position-metadata queries during transcript mirror recovery [#146684](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146684).
- With process diagnostics and warning logging enabled, distinguish queue wait from provider time for settled session-catalog requests taking at least one second [#148047](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/148047). Thanks @giangthb. These diagnostics identify delays rather than repair them; provider hashes support correlation, not anonymization.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Work with large Workboards">

Opening a Workboard and claiming a task now involve less unrelated card data, so damaged records elsewhere no longer block those actions. Other application work can continue while Workboard waits on its database, and plugin reload lets already-accepted storage work finish. Damaged records still need attention when read directly, and archived cards still prevent a board from being deleted.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Read Workboard statistics and board occupancy without loading full cards [#146237](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146237).
- Claim Workboard tasks without decoding unrelated card history [#146245](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146245).
- Load only the selected Workboard board and isolate unrelated corruption [#146683](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146683).
- Read only the selected Workboard card for notifications [#146748](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146748).
- Run Workboard database operations in a background worker and drain admitted work during reload [#146976](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146976).
- Check task prerequisites without loading complete parent cards [#147102](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147102).
- Load only matching board or card notification subscriptions, isolating malformed subscriptions outside the selected scope [#147780](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147780).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Save large Team Reports">

Other work can continue while Team Reports waits on its database to save a report. Large roster refreshes use fewer writes, and a failed refresh preserves the complete previous report and roster. Standalone plugin installations need a compatible host with the SQLite worker SDK.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Keep Team Reports database waits off the Gateway thread [#144871](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144871).
- Save large Team Reports with fewer writes and unchanged rollback protection [#146233](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146233).
- Read Team Reports status and health warnings without transferring unused report Markdown [#147452](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147452).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Maintain Logbook without blocking the Gateway">

Logbook handles database waits in the background so other application work can continue. Screenshot cleanup waits until active previews and analysis finish reading the images. Expired screenshot metadata is removed in batches while timeline cards, observations and standups remain available under the existing retention rules.

Use a compatible worker-capable host for standalone Logbook installations. Stop Logbook and let active work finish before moving its storage. Rolling back the database cannot restore screenshot files that retention already deleted.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Move Logbook database work off-thread and coordinate screenshot cleanup [#144982](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144982).
- Batch expired Logbook screenshot metadata cleanup [#146306](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146306).
- Transfer metadata only for Logbook's selected screenshot sample, retaining the same limit of 16 images and existing retention behavior [#147999](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147999).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Search saved meeting and call history">

Find past meetings by words in saved notes or transcripts, then read long summaries with their headings and lists intact. The wider meeting timeline gives those notes more room, with clear loading feedback on desktop and mobile. Larger saved transcript libraries and voice-call histories also avoid repeated database work when you reopen them.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Search saved meeting notes and transcripts and read formatted long summaries [#145367](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145367).
- Check meeting transcript retries without loading all utterance revisions [#146381](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146381).
- Reuse payload-size calculations when loading saved transcript libraries [#146623](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146623).
- Batch saved voice-call history reads to reduce database overhead [#146625](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146625).

**Bug fixes**

- Save Google Meet artifact and attendance summaries to the requested output file, including the default format [#147078](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147078).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Keep Reef message checks available">

[Reef](/channels/reef) can reuse an explicitly configured OpenAI OAuth login for message checks, with the required model permissions and compatible Codex runtime policy. Existing API-key setups remain supported. If message-tracking storage fills, the shared inbox stays connected and parks affected entries until capacity becomes available.

An entry may be delivered again if its earlier delivery could not be recorded. Before downgrading from the OAuth setup to an older version, restore the older API-key configuration described in the Reef guide.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Reuse OpenAI OAuth for Reef message checks [#134815](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/134815). Thanks @jason-allen-oneal, @patrick-erichsen.
- Reuse peer reads within Reef overdue-delivery and rejection scans for backlogs targeting the same peers [#147505](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147505).

**Bug fixes**

- Keep Reef inbox processing available during tracking-storage exhaustion [#144637](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144637). Thanks @eleqtrizit.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Read original timestamps in Fleet logs">

Add `--timestamps` to `openclaw fleet logs` to compare container events by their original log time. It works alongside `--follow`, `--tail` and `--since`; leave it out to keep the existing output format.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Add original container timestamps to Fleet logs [#132554](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/132554). Thanks @alix-007, @patrick-erichsen.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Diagnose MCP configuration and cleanup">

Read-only [Doctor checks](/cli/doctor/lint) now leave schema checks for OAuth-backed MCP servers to authenticated operations that can save refreshed credentials, preserving working logins. Use `--severity-min info` to see which checks were deferred. Check a configured server with `openclaw mcp probe <name>` using its serving configuration; plugin-provided servers and agent-local profiles need an authenticated serving-agent turn. Already-invalid logins still need reauthorization, and non-OAuth checks continue.

MCP diagnostics now reject folders mistakenly used as server commands and preserve Doctor findings when a failing server also has trouble shutting down. Affected tools can return valid results with nested schema references without a false validation failure. When no saved servers are enabled, `openclaw mcp probe` explains how to add or enable one. If cleanup remains unconfirmed, inspect or stop the affected MCP processes before rerunning Doctor.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Contain MCP cleanup failures and preserve Doctor findings [#144941](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144941). Thanks @libraho, @itanyplus.
- Reject folders used as MCP server commands [#144998](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144998). Thanks @masatohoshino.
- Accept valid MCP results with nested schema references [#145533](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145533).
- Retain the original cause of uncertain MCP proxy cleanup [#146561](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146561).
- Explain MCP probes with no enabled servers and show add or enable guidance [#147216](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147216).
- Preserve MCP OAuth logins by deferring read-only Doctor probes to authenticated operations that can save refreshed credentials [#147454](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147454). Thanks @shad0wca7.
- Accept cleanup of already-ended MCP HTTP sessions without false warnings; authentication, server and local-close errors still surface [#145831](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145831). Thanks @zhangguiping-xydt, @baumus, @obviyus. A server masking another failure as a DELETE 404 remains indistinguishable from an ended session.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Protect current workflow progress">

An outdated task-flow update can no longer replace newer saved progress or recreate a deleted flow. Progress notifications also wait until the update commits, so a rolled-back change does not appear to have succeeded.

Finished subagent tasks and their linked flows also avoid redundant updates during idle Gateway restarts. Genuine corrections still persist, and failed notifications can still retry.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Preserve current task-flow progress against stale updates [#145699](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145699).
- Keep unchanged finished subagent tasks and linked flow revisions stable across idle Gateway restarts [#145940](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145940). Thanks @zhangguiping-xydt, @anusha0501, @obviyus, @eliasbogordos.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Adopt asynchronous plugin storage">

Plugin authors can read tasks and flows asynchronously and save managed-flow progress while other application work continues during database waits. Webhooks and Lobster wait for those saves before returning results or starting work that depends on them. Plugins and Webhooks can also await linking already-started child work to a managed flow. These APIs are optional, and existing synchronous APIs remain supported. The [keyed-storage migration guide](/plugins/sdk-runtime/state-and-system#synchronous-keyed-store-migration) explains how to switch interfaces without copying data.

Check host compatibility before using new helpers, await writes, and reread current state before retrying a creation whose outcome is uncertain. Returned snapshots still need the documented revision and ownership checks before later changes.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Avoid redundant retention scans during eligible plugin-state writes [#144198](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144198). Thanks @tedbarnett.
- Reuse compiled queries for plugin-state saves and Doctor imports [#144206](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144206).
- Expose the selected plugin runtime entry [#144818](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144818).
- Package declared private plugin workers [#144821](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144821).
- Avoid unnecessary path work during repeated plugin API loads [#145443](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145443).
- Reuse compiled queries for repeated task and flow reads [#145716](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145716).
- Add optional asynchronous task and flow reads for plugins [#145851](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145851).
- Skip unnecessary plugin-state expiry scans [#146114](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146114).
- Avoid loading old plugin payloads during writes and repeated imports [#146278](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146278).
- Count plugin records without loading their full contents [#146285](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146285).
- Use lightweight counts for plugin capacity checks and migration previews [#146377](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146377).
- Save managed workflow progress through awaited background writes [#146495](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146495).
- Count workflow tasks without loading complete task records [#146682](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146682).
- Link already-started child tasks to managed flows through an awaited API [#146678](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146678).
- Read only enough old plugin-cache entries to make room for a new artifact [#146750](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146750).
- Move awaited plugin inventory and installation-record database reads off the application thread while retaining synchronous helpers and existing writes [#148017](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/148017).

**Limits and compatibility**

- Plan migration from synchronous plugin storage using the same stored data [#145158](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145158).

**Bug fixes**

- Reduce memory needed to prepare large plugin inputs [#145659](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145659). Thanks @fuller-stack-dev.
- Return complete large SQLite worker results [#145926](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145926).
- Improve async task storage opening and cleanup [#146172](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146172).

**Documentation**

- Correct managed-flow async migration and retry guidance [#146568](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146568).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Browse and filter Workboard tasks">

Workboard filters now work together, so you can narrow cards by status, priority, attention needed and recent completion, including on mobile. Switch from the default board to a list with aligned task details and collapsible status groups when you want to scan more work at once. The list-view choice is not saved. Clearing filters keeps your selected agent context; use the agent picker to return to All agents.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Combine Workboard filters, use compact mobile controls, and clear an agent scope after roster changes [#144751](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144751). Thanks @vyctorbrzezowski.
- Browse Workboard in a responsive list with collapsible status groups [#144753](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144753). Thanks @vyctorbrzezowski.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Edit Workboard cards and protect drafts">

Workboard card details now organize task information, activity and recent session context into tabs, with common fields editable in place. You can also rename a board and choose or reset its icon and color. Closing changed cards or comments asks before discarding your draft, and failed saves keep your edits available. Dragging cards preserves neighboring status changes, while attachments on a recreated card survive cleanup of its predecessor.

Use matching current Workboard, Gateway and Control UI components for these controls. If a move's result is uncertain, wait for a successful reload before editing again; your open comment draft remains available.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Show clearer card and session outcomes and preserve concurrent neighboring changes during drag moves [#144752](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144752). Thanks @vyctorbrzezowski.
- Edit board appearance and protect unsaved card and comment drafts [#144754](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144754). Thanks @vyctorbrzezowski.
- Organize card details into tabs with inline editing, session previews and automation context [#144755](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144755). Thanks @vyctorbrzezowski.

**Bug fixes**

- Preserve new attachments when a deleted session card is recreated during cleanup [#147191](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147191).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Change several Workboard cards at once">

Select several Workboard cards in board or list view to change their status, assignment, priority or labels together, or to archive or delete them. A batch can finish only partly, so review the results before retrying the unfinished cards that remain selected. Deleting a card leaves its running session active. These controls require matching current Workboard and Gateway components.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Select multiple Workboard cards for common changes and review partial results before retrying [#144756](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144756). Thanks @vyctorbrzezowski.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Finish background plugin work">

Deferred plugin webhook callbacks can finish after the request that started them ends, while remaining tracked during Gateway shutdown. This repairs the closed-request failure that could interrupt background processing. Plugin authors using the [deferred-work helper](/plugins/sdk-overview/infrastructure) should account for callbacks having their own lifetime and continuing after the requester cancels.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Keep deferred webhook work alive after its initiating request ends [#144801](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144801). Thanks @liuwqgit, @maxwell-code07, @vincentkoc.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Find and combine tools in Code Mode">

With Code Mode enabled, an agent can find native and MCP tools together by describing the task it needs to perform. Optional TypeScript checks help it combine calls by describing the results each automation or process action can return, including failures. The checks report several errors at once and reuse unchanged compiler inputs. Existing permissions and approvals still apply. If a tool returns malformed data, the error identifies problem fields and advises checking what happened before retrying, because the action may already have run.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**
