- Find enabled native and MCP tools together by task, with exact callable details [#147134](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147134).
- Add typed automation and process results, node declarations and grouped compiler diagnostics [#147143](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147143).
- Reuse unchanged compiler inputs for repeated checked TypeScript cells [#147252](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147252).
- Match checked automation and process result types to the requested action, retaining applicable failure results and broader types when needed [#147291](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147291).

**Bug fixes**

- Identify malformed tool-result fields and advise checking effects before retrying [#147239](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147239).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Continue coding-app conversations after Stop">

Stopping a reply now keeps an established coding-app conversation available with its earlier context for the next turn. The partial reply stays visible, and the interrupted turn remains unsuccessful, so you can continue without treating unfinished work as complete.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Preserve established CLI conversation context after cancellation and restore interrupted fork preparation [#147139](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147139). Thanks @obviyus, @peetiegonzalez, @marvinthebored, @enotionz, @ataraxiat.
- Report unexpected server signal exits in established ACP client sessions as failure with status 1; deliberate signal quit succeeds and numeric server exit codes still propagate [#148027](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/148027).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Reuse results within a Code Mode run">

Code Mode can [save a result and reuse its complete data](/tools/code-mode/guest-api) in later cells, including after wait and resume, without fetching it again. Eligible oversized final objects and arrays can also become reusable references automatically during interactive execution. A preview is only a sample; load the saved result for calculations over the full dataset.

These are temporary snapshots that expire when the run ends or aborts, tools or permissions change, or the Gateway restarts. The store holds up to 64 entries with a default 10 MiB encoded-JSON allowance, and refuses new saves when full without evicting existing results. Result operations are unavailable in restart-safe cells. [Automatic retention](/tools/code-mode/output) also excludes headless execution and can fall back to ordinary truncation when the result or reference cannot fit.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Save, load and delete complete JSON results across cells within one Code Mode run [#147154](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147154).
- Automatically retain eligible oversized interactive results with reusable references and sampled previews [#147278](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147278).
- Avoid repeated encoded-byte counting when fitting large OpenClaw Code Mode results into responses [#147718](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147718).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="List 1Password items efficiently">

In installations with many agents, 1Password item listings can skip unrelated access grants and avoid failures caused by damage in those grants. This requires a storage adapter that supports reading only the requesting agent's relevant grants. Listings still return metadata, with existing secret-access and approval rules in place.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Fetch relevant access grants for 1Password item listings without enumerating every agent's grants [#147183](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147183).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Recover a custom plugin workspace">

UI customization now lives under Plugins → Customize UI, replacing the floating button. The Plugins page always opens the built-in workspace so you can reach these controls even when a custom workspace hides navigation. Open `/plugins` under your Control UI base URL and choose Built-in to return to the standard workspace.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Move customization to Plugins and keep its built-in workspace available for recovery [#147245](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147245).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Retrieve files delivered by a specific run">

Integrations can request only the files an assistant delivered for a chosen run, leaving out user uploads and raw tool observations. Set `messageRole` to `assistant` together with `runId`; the run ID alone does not select outputs. Scoped managed downloads return the exact selected file or report it missing when its ID is stale.

Older Gateways reject the new filter. Integrations must show that limitation instead of silently retrying without it.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Select assistant-delivered files by run and preserve exact file identity in scoped managed downloads [#147682](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147682). Thanks @vincentkoc.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Keep registered diff previews during cleanup">

Diffs cleanup now preserves an older preview directory if it becomes registered while its age is being checked. It also skips unnecessary database checks for recent files. Files being rendered remain protected, and orphan cleanup still applies only to directories more than 24 hours old.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Preserve diff preview directories registered during the age check and skip recent-file database lookups [#147624](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147624).

</details>

</Accordion>

</AccordionGroup>

## Security and Privacy

These changes tighten specific permission checks and improve how OpenClaw handles credentials in tool results and diagnostics. Client developers can also add checks that tie actions to the selected account.

<AccordionGroup>

<Accordion title="Keep command approval tied to the launch">

OpenClaw checks saved command permissions again just before launch, so revoking approval can stop a command that is still waiting to start. Reviewed commands also retain their approved program paths through supported shell execution. Revocation does not stop a process that has already started.

If you use custom Homebrew `curl` or `git` paths, move `HOMEBREW_CURL_PATH` and `HOMEBREW_GIT_PATH` from the launch-directory `.env` into your launching shell or `~/.openclaw/.env`. Project environment files can no longer choose those executables during an approved dependency install.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Security and trust**

- Block project .env files from choosing Homebrew curl and git executables [#146389](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146389). Thanks @eleqtrizit.
- Recheck persisted command permission at launch and bind reviewed programs [#146517](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146517).

**Bug fixes**

- Give automatic command reviews enough output space to finish [#144587](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144587). Thanks @masterswords1 and @unknownengineofficial.
- Match verified Gateway profiles in command allowlists [#144601](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144601). Thanks @jamesy023 and @obviyus.

**Improvements**

- Reduce repeated work when checking large saved command-approval lists, without caching permission decisions between checks [#146773](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146773).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Keep sandbox writes inside their allowed paths">

Sandboxed Codex file changes through OpenClaw's shipped local and remote bridges now reject directory links that redirect them into protected locations. Authorized directory aliases still work. If Doctor cannot quarantine a damaged legacy sandbox registry file, it keeps the original available for recovery. The updated [patch-tool guide](/tools/apply-patch#notes) explains why full command access can still leave file changes restricted.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Security and trust**

- Prevent symlinks from redirecting sandbox file changes into protected paths [#143501](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/143501). Thanks @eleqtrizit.

**Bug fixes**

- Preserve sandbox registry files when quarantine fails [#144787](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144787). Thanks @alix-007 and @obviyus.

**Documentation**

- Explain patch-tool containment, session-policy precedence and worker differences. Full execution or session mode does not bypass required roots or sandbox restrictions; this documents existing permissions [#136088](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/136088). Thanks @marvinthebored, @peetiegonzalez and @obviyus.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Mask recognized secrets in tool results and diagnostics">

OpenClaw's built-in agent runtime now masks recognized and registered secrets in completed tool-result text before the next model request, including file reads, command output, and tool errors. With redaction enabled, custom rules retain built-in credential checks. The release also corrects mistaken masking of public status links and masks reflected bot credentials in new Mattermost error diagnostics.

Log views and exports now mask recognized credentials and private-key fragments across selected portions of stored logs while keeping JSON readable. The stored files remain unchanged, and reading a selection can take longer because masking may scan earlier history. Secret detection remains limited to recognized patterns and registered values, and the tool-result change does not cover native Codex. Previously shortened message text cannot be restored.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Security and trust**

- Redact local paths in sidebar failure previews [#143907](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/143907). Thanks @romneyda.
- Mask reflected Mattermost credentials in error diagnostics [#144172](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144172). Thanks @wangmiao0668000666 and @obviyus.
- Preserve public URLs and credential masking with custom redaction rules, including masked token hints in diagnostic exports. With redaction enabled, custom rules retain built-in structural credential protection [#145810](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145810). Thanks @obviyus and @schjonhaug.
- Mask recognized tool-result secrets before they reach live model context [#146596](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146596). Thanks @nicknmorty.
- Mask private-key fragments across stored-log selections while preserving readable records and original file-byte cursors [#146850](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146850). Thanks @obviyus.
- Keep registered secrets masked across source and bundled module copies in the same process [#147316](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147316).

**Bug fixes**

- Preserve public status URLs when masking credentials in chat [#145553](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145553). Thanks @obviyus and @schjonhaug.
- Keep newly written JSON logs readable when quoted credentials are masked [#146718](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146718). Thanks @obviyus.

**Improvements**

- Avoid unrelated secret reads during GitHub maintenance [#144940](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144940).
- Reduce diagnostic redaction allocations [#146192](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146192).
- Skip unrelated saved-credential metadata during temporary GitHub credential cleanup [#146882](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146882). Thanks @vincentkoc.
- Avoid repeatedly rebuilding JSON log records between masking rules while preserving output and rule order. Small-record timings remain mixed [#148007](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/148007).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Honor current tool and messaging permissions">

Limited tool profiles now also restrict [Codex native shell and file access](/plugins/codex-harness). Creating or editing messaging topics follows the configured rules for acting across conversations and providers, and workspace plugins need [explicit trust](/tools/plugin) before replacing configured channels. Stopping or removing a worker also interrupts its active workspace downloads when teardown revokes access.

Previously bypassing workflows may now be refused. Keep restricted Codex sessions on a compatible managed runtime, and explicitly permit any intended cross-context topic management. [PDF access](/tools/pdf) now follows media and OpenClaw group restrictions, so an explicit PDF allow cannot override a conflicting group deny. Narrow that deny if PDF access is intended.

Cross-service sends can also be refused when OpenClaw knows the source service but has lost the conversation address, including after restart recovery or a subagent continuation. Existing global or per-agent `tools.message.crossContext.allowAcrossProviders` opt-ins still apply.

Doctor also stops copying LINE direct-message senders into group permissions. Review existing `groupAllowFrom` entries if you ran an earlier repair, since previously copied entries remain in place.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Security and trust**

- Apply Teams route restrictions to reactions and conflicting activity scopes [#143735](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/143735). Thanks @pgondhi987.
- Interrupt active workspace downloads when worker access is revoked [#144640](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144640). Thanks @eleqtrizit.
- Reject another agent's saved credentials after restore. Keep the restored database's recorded agent identity or initialize separate agent state for local embedding and media commands [#145692](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145692). Thanks @obviyus.
- Apply limited tool profiles to Codex native execution [#145694](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145694).
- Require explicit trust before workspace plugins replace configured channels [#146352](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146352). Thanks @eleqtrizit.
- Enforce cross-context permissions when creating or editing messaging topics [#146409](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146409). Thanks @eleqtrizit.
- Enforce restricted child-agent scheduler permissions through managed MCP, including the cron alias [#146671](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146671). Thanks @eleqtrizit.
- Keep LINE direct-message and group permissions separate during Doctor repair and explain missing group sender lists [#147135](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147135). Thanks @obviyus and @edenfunf.
- Apply current session visibility to individual message reads and reject stale results after session replacement. Reload the conversation when this returns a retryable error [#147330](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147330). Thanks @fuller-stack-dev.
- Enforce cross-provider messaging restrictions when the source service is known but its conversation target is missing, including recovery and subagent continuation. Command-line requests without a source provider remain unbound [#147939](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147939).

**Bug fixes**

- Keep delayed Tailscale profile pictures attached to the original profile [#145180](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145180).
- Keep intentional open-group policies while retaining update warnings [#145379](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145379). Thanks @donniefi and @patrykpatryk5.
- Recognize PDF in built-in diagnostics and media/OpenClaw group policies [#146408](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146408). Thanks @bartok9 and @mnowrot.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Apply network limits to downloads and incoming webhooks">

Browser download actions now follow the selected profile's destination restrictions, so downloads that previously bypassed those rules can be rejected. Nextcloud Talk and Feishu webhook listeners also limit simultaneous incomplete requests before authentication, reducing the resources those requests can hold open.

At saturation, legitimate webhook arrivals can receive a closed `429` response and may be lost. Reduce or buffer upstream concurrency where needed, and do not assume rejected deliveries will retry. The [Nextcloud Talk guide](/channels/nextcloud-talk) explains its fixed read limit and overload behavior.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Security and trust**

- Update IMAP's Nodemailer dependency for reported security advisories [#145477](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145477). Thanks @romneyda.
- Apply browser destination restrictions to explicit and waited downloads [#146297](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146297). Thanks @eleqtrizit.
- Limit Nextcloud Talk to 64 simultaneous unauthenticated webhook reads while preserving earlier acknowledgements [#146356](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146356). Thanks @eleqtrizit.
- Limit Feishu to 64 simultaneous unauthenticated webhook body reads per account route [04644af](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/04644af394011ab7ea15c31833360e7132d3053f). Thanks @alix-007. Related proposal [#137230](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/137230).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Replace Gateway certificates without disconnecting clients">

If your Gateway serves HTTPS directly, you can replace certificate files at their existing paths without restarting listeners or disconnecting established clients. Incomplete or mismatched replacements leave the last accepted certificate in use. Turning automatic reload off pauses certificate adoption until you enable it again.

Update saved certificate pins on remote clients before reconnecting after renewal. Changing TLS enablement, certificate paths, or listener configuration still requires a restart. See [hot reload](/gateway/configuration/hot-reload) for the supported behavior.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Renew Gateway TLS certificates without disconnecting established clients [#146267](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146267).

**Bug fixes**

- Stop the TLS certificate-renewal watcher during Gateway shutdown [#146412](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146412).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Upload files through protected network connections">

File uploads through [protected egress](/gateway/secrets/secret-store-and-egress) now send the correct upload length to services that require it, while retaining authorized secret substitution. This applies to uploads with a known length up to 100 MiB. Cancelling a buffered upload stops its unsent remainder, though bytes already submitted cannot be recalled.

These uploads wait until OpenClaw has collected the full body before sending. Shared capacity limits or the five-minute deadline can refuse a transfer. Larger uploads and those without a known length still send in chunks, which some destinations reject.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Preserve fixed-length uploads up to 100 MiB through protected egress after authorized secret substitution, with existing destination and TLS checks [#144902](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144902). Thanks @iwhatsskill and @vacinc.
- Stop remaining buffered upload writes when the run is revoked, the proxy stops, or the client disconnects [#146840](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146840). Thanks @vincentkoc.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Protect workspace files during cancellation">

Cancelling during preparation can now stop pending workspace writes, edits, and directory changes, with permission checked again before the covered changes are made. Worker attachments copy within their allowed size without overwriting files from earlier turns. Cancelling before an attachment is published leaves no new destination file; attachments already published, including any replacements you later saved, stay in place. [Managed worktrees](/concepts/managed-worktrees) also preserve supported files while rejecting source files replaced with symlinks.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Security and trust**

- Guard cancelled workspace changes and memory appends, bound attachment copying, and preserve managed-worktree file modes, supported hardlinks, large files, and literal tilde directory names. Backup capture avoids blocking on substituted special files, bundle checks use the admitted file's bytes, and Control UI health checks retain their file-size limit [#147138](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147138).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Configure the approval reviewer">

You can now set the [approval reviewer's reasoning and optional Fast mode](/tools/exec) independently of the main agent, globally or per agent. Leaving the options unset keeps provider defaults, and existing approval checks and human fallback remain in place. The controls apply to OpenClaw's model-backed reviews, not native Codex Guardian.

Reasoning choices can affect cost, waiting time, and review quality. Fast mode requests priority processing on supported OpenAI Responses and ChatGPT/OAuth routes and may cost more; other providers may ignore it. If you enable these options, remove the new `thinking` and `fastMode` keys before downgrading to a version that does not recognize them.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Configure command and widget approval-review reasoning and Fast mode independently of the main agent [#147192](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147192). Thanks @vacinc.
- Translate approval-review reasoning and Fast-mode labels and help in 20 Control UI locales [#147213](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147213). Thanks @vincentkoc.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Use correctly owned FreeBSD credentials">

The FreeBSD desktop now rejects Gateway token and password files owned by another user. If this affects your connection, correct the file ownership to match the account running the app or replace the credentials in Settings before reconnecting. Rejected files leave your saved settings intact.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Security and trust**

- Require FreeBSD Gateway credential files to belong to the app user, matching the existing Linux check [#147212](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147212). Thanks @vincentkoc and @lucarinaorg.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Use Git through protected network connections">

If you use protected egress, Git commands launched by the Gateway now receive the proxy's trusted certificate bundle. This fixes the resulting HTTPS certificate error while keeping certificate verification enabled, with no new settings required.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Give Gateway-launched Git HTTPS commands the protected-egress certificate bundle through Git's own trust setting [#147410](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147410).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Bind client actions to the selected account">

Client developers can now add [selected-account checks](/gateway/protocol/transport) so their apps detect when the chosen account no longer matches the signed-in profile. Already accepted work keeps its original conversation and receipt through ordinary disconnects or later account selection changes. These checks are opt-in; other requests keep their existing behavior.

If a client requires these checks, it must report when the Gateway does not support them. An account mismatch reported after an action starts does not mean the action was undone. Before retrying, check earlier acknowledgements or receipts and keep the original idempotency key, which identifies the request across retries.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Add optional `expectedProfileId` requests and `recipientProfileId` event metadata through `profile-binding-v1`. Use the exact profile ID from `users.self` and accept events only for the matching selected profile and physical Gateway connection [#143587](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/143587). Thanks @vincentkoc.

</details>

</Accordion>

</AccordionGroup>

## Quality-of-Life Improvements

Everyday terminal use gains image previews and clearer feedback, while managed worktrees can share source storage across more platforms. Background history loading helps OpenClaw stay responsive while opening large conversations.

<AccordionGroup>

<Accordion title="See images in compatible terminal chats">

Received and generated images now appear as static previews in Ghostty, iTerm2, and Kitty, including supported images in reopened conversations and tool cards. [Terminal image previews](/web/tui#image-previews) show up to four images per message and keep the 24 most recent previews. Unsupported terminals retain readable text, and previews are off by default inside tmux and GNU Screen.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Display static image attachments inline in compatible terminals [#146644](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146644).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Keep terminal controls and output clear">

Ctrl+C hints no longer hide an ongoing response's activity indicator. In the Control UI, closing a connecting terminal tab keeps it closed, and Retry stays attached to the failed tab even when another tab connects or exits.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Reduce repeated formatting work for long terminal health details [#145401](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145401).
- Show shell sessions and controls in the Chat panel header [#145640](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145640).
- Reduce scrollback overhead during heavy small-write logging [#145687](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145687).
- Format long Windows CLI tables with less path-scanning work [#146307](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146307).

**Bug fixes**

- Default omitted shell platform arguments to the local host [#133752](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/133752). Thanks @xialonglee, @gracy769, and @patrick-erichsen.
- Keep transcript command output free of startup notes [#144579](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144579). Thanks @bruce-yii and @obviyus.
- Keep terminal Retry actions paired with the right failed tab [#145222](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145222).
- Keep cancelled connecting terminal tabs closed [#145452](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145452).
- Reject extra empty CLI arguments instead of silently ignoring later input [#145468](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145468).
- Preserve final command output during Bun event-loop stalls [#145564](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145564).
- Restore interactive exec under Bun on non-Windows hosts through the existing Node terminal helper, which requires Node [#145749](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145749).
- Clarify invalid command working directories under Bun [#145852](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145852).
- Keep terminal activity visible while showing Ctrl+C hints [#146367](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146367).
- Keep replacement task terminals open when an older task finishes, and allow failed task notifications to be sent on a later update [#146712](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146712).
- Restore PowerShell command and option-value suggestions when editing before arguments already on the command line [#147737](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147737).
- Resolve explicit relative working directories for local commands from the session workspace, keeping `~` literal and `..` parent-relative [#148162](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/148162).
- Return one complete JSON error with exit code 1 when shared configuration validation stops a JSON-mode command, including large issue lists [#145768](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145768). Thanks @alix-007 and @obviyus.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Share source storage across managed worktrees">

[Managed worktrees](/concepts/managed-worktrees) can now share unchanged source-file storage on eligible Linux Btrfs, macOS APFS, and Windows ReFS or Dev Drive destinations, while keeping each checkout's edits and setup independent. Sharing is selected automatically on supported destinations. Repeated APFS checkouts avoid the initial file-by-file cloning slowdown, and worktree removal saves recoverable tracked and untracked edits with less delay.

The first source template and each checkout's dependencies still need preparation. ReFS sharing can take longer than ordinary Git checkout; set [`worktreeAcceleration` to `false`](/gateway/config-runtime) to opt out. Btrfs sharing requires the optional native package; unavailable or disabled native helpers use ordinary Git and its normal space checks. APFS uses ordinary Git when inherited permissions cannot be preserved or checked, and cancelling an already-started native clone waits for it to finish.

Cancelling workspace cleanup on a worker node now stops deletions that have not started and leaves those items available for a later cleanup attempt. A deletion already underway finishes and cannot be undone.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Accelerate repeated Linux/Btrfs worktrees with shared source snapshots [#146251](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146251).
- Share managed-worktree source storage on macOS APFS [#146338](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146338).
- Share Windows managed-worktree source storage on ReFS and Dev Drive [#146403](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146403).
- Remove redundant Git queries and elapsed APFS safety waits [#146722](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146722).
- Speed managed worktree removal and reduce reusable-clone space requirements [#146723](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146723).
- Restore saved worktrees and create repeated checkouts with less preparation while retaining saved edits [#146765](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146765).
- List managed worktrees without loading unused provisioning manifests [#146890](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146890).
- Reduce pauses during APFS worktree preparation, use native Btrfs sharing without the btrfs command, and skip unnecessary disk syncs for temporary installation files [#146906](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146906).
- Show which cleanup stages took time in slow worktree-removal logs, with diagnostics and info logging enabled, after the operation settles [#147379](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147379).

**Bug fixes**

- Speed up repeated APFS worktree creation while retaining shared source storage [#146401](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146401).
- Let new worktrees prepare alongside unrelated cleanup inspection, while retaining waits for shared disk allocation [#147358](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147358). Thanks @vacinc.
- Preserve saved worktree snapshot files after failed registry deletion when using a state directory different from the default [#147922](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147922).
- Stop further node workspace cleanup after cancellation while allowing an already-started deletion to finish [#147616](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147616).
- Retry recognized transient failures in managed Git reads and project clones once after one second, within the original timeout; pushes, pulls, authentication failures, and arbitrary agent or setup commands are excluded [#147692](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147692).

**Security and trust**

- Preserve inherited permissions when creating APFS managed worktrees [#146586](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146586).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Keep goal instructions and budgets intact">

Goals retain multiline instructions and meaningful spacing, so a carefully written objective reaches the agent intact. Goals can also be created without a token budget, and reconfirming completion keeps the original finish time.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Preserve goal instructions and optional budgets [#145041](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145041).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Avoid repeated local processing">

OpenClaw does less repeated work when preparing long conversations, showing progress, and running agents in parallel. It makes fewer temporary copies without changing the conversation content, and requests for recent history, usage, or selected agent results skip older details they do not need.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Reduce repeated processing of long progress messages [#145149](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145149).
- Reduce temporary copies when preparing long conversation history [#145265](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145265).
- Reduce temporary allocations during parallel agent work [#145630](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145630).
- Reduce temporary allocations during agent turns [#146037](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146037).
- Use less memory for quiet BOOT.md runs [#146041](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146041).
- Reduce temporary allocations during parallel Gateway work [#146219](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146219).
- Avoid unnecessary temporary work during parallel agent sessions [#146344](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146344).
- Reuse hashing helpers and larger reads for file verification [#146581](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146581).
- Skip unnecessary delivery-instruction parsing when saving code-heavy replies [#146858](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146858).
- Read less unused history information for small recent-history and usage requests [#147205](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147205).
- Avoid loading old subagent task and result bodies during chat polling, session updates, and waits for selected agents [#147209](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147209).
- Reduce repeated work when matching saved chat messages [#147210](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147210).
- Avoid duplicate database copies within one CLI plugin-metadata operation, with newer changes visible to the next operation [#147861](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147861). Thanks @pfrederiksen.
- Batch large cleanups of stale usage summaries while preserving concurrent updates and retention rules; small cleanups can take longer [#147998](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147998).

**Bug fixes**

- Keep valid setup recommendations and transcript summaries when diagnostic text follows the JSON result, while still rejecting malformed or invalid results [#145764](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145764). Thanks @zengwen-dt and @vincentkoc.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Preview QA Lab files">

In QA Lab, recognized file formats determine the preview, so a descriptive label containing a word such as “gif” no longer hides a log or misidentifies a JSON file.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Choose QA Lab previews from recognized file formats [#145612](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145612).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Run more conversations at once">

Installations using the [default concurrency limit](/concepts/queue) can run more independent conversations while others wait for model or tool responses. The default now allows four main agent runs per available CPU, with at least eight slots—32 on an eight-CPU host and 192 on a 48-CPU host. Each conversation still runs one turn at a time, and subagent and scheduled-job limits remain separate.

More simultaneous turns can use more memory and put more pressure on providers. Existing [`agents.defaults.maxConcurrent` settings](/gateway/config-agents/models) still take precedence. Set a lower explicit limit for a constrained machine or resource-heavy work.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Increase default capacity for independent conversations while preserving explicit limits and one turn at a time per conversation [#147423](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147423).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Keep the Gateway responsive during history loads">

Opening large conversations or loading history in several clients leaves the Gateway more time to respond to controls and other requests. [Saved-history preparation](/reference/session-management-compaction/store) now runs in the background, with session and access checks before history is shown. If the background reader is busy, unavailable, or times out, you receive an error and can retry.

The tradeoff is more memory use and potentially longer waits for history. The warm background worker added about 270 MiB in the reported four-CPU Linux test. First reads can be slower, including after the worker shuts down while idle, and separate requests can use more CPU. Even small history pages can occasionally take longer to load.

Opening the branch list in a large saved conversation also uses a separate background worker, leaving the Gateway available for other requests. Incognito branch lists stay local. In a separate synthetic test, this worker added about 105 MiB of process memory and took about 0.42 seconds for its first request.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Prepare saved conversation history in the background so large or concurrent reads leave more time for Gateway controls [#147363](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147363). Thanks @datrab.
- Prepare stored-conversation branch summaries in the background while preserving branch order, selection, and contents [#147649](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147649).

**Bug fixes**

- Avoid unnecessary scans for sparse conversation-history markers when SQLite statistics favor a slower lookup, without requiring a new index or user action [#148057](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/148057).

</details>

</Accordion>

</AccordionGroup>

## Other Bug Fixes

Interrupted conversations and delegated tasks get more ways to finish from saved work, while explicit Stop requests remain cancelled.

<AccordionGroup>

<Accordion title="Subagent results and recovery">

Ordinary subagents can use completed tool results before pausing. When they resume and finish, the parent conversation can continue. Copilot child tasks also preserve their original outcome for another save attempt after a temporary storage failure. Later parent turns can find retained child results still awaiting delivery or continuation. A child you explicitly stop stays cancelled.

In OpenClaw's built-in agent runtime, the system expert can now answer even when only one ordinary agent is allowed to run at a time. Cancelling the parent task can still leave queued expert work able to start.

If a subagent was already paused before upgrading, send it a follow-up to resume it. Abandoned tasks cleared by maintenance follow the existing retention rules. Downgrading does not restore their earlier status or records already removed by retention. If cleanup exhausts its retries, investigate the warning; that cleanup remains unfinished.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Preserve project and task context in visible child sessions [#145102](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145102).
- Preserve Copilot child outcomes after temporary task-save failures [#145467](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145467).
- Release completed caller state from idle background workers [#145605](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145605).
- Clear abandoned subagent activity after restart cleanup [#146248](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146248). Thanks @vacinc.
- Preserve queued Swarm callers and protect reassigned sessions from cleanup [#146490](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146490).
- Read current Incognito and secondary-agent completion and preserve replacement sessions during cleanup [#146542](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146542).
- Protect reset subagent sessions from stale completed-run cleanup; a reset can fail while completion settles or protection cannot be saved, and saved protection remains if a later reset step fails [#146565](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146565).
- Recover paused subagents, preserve parent continuation, and show nested sessions and task names [#146667](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146667).
- Keep explicitly stopped child runs cancelled [#146711](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146711).
- Preserve paused-task continuation, settle expired cancellation bookkeeping, and avoid impossible recovery sends and unchanged chat-catalog reloads [#146736](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146736).
- Let later parent turns discover recent completed children and retained results still awaiting delivery or continuation, with marked size limits; reading them does not acknowledge or retry delivery [#112623](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/112623). Thanks @ericcaiwx-star, @fuller-stack-dev, and eric.
- Settle yielded Swarm collectors so waiting callers receive their result or missing-output error; previously stranded records are not recovered [#146716](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146716). Thanks @etzelm, @vacinc, and @potterdigital.
- Avoid duplicate follow-up turns after a parent receives its visible child's reply inline, while preserving bounded late-reply continuation [#147394](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147394). Thanks @obviyus, @ylcn91, and @tonyisblind.
- Restore validated structured results for CLI-backed Swarm collectors; collectors must submit through the required result tool and no longer receive `sessions_yield`, `ask_user`, or `sessions_send` [#146715](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146715). Thanks @etzelm, @vacinc, and @potterdigital.
- Keep long-running subagents counted for activity, concurrency limits, and parent waiting when local execution or an exact local queue reservation can be verified [#147431](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147431). Thanks @fuller-stack-dev.
- Keep failed cleanup within its retry delay and current-process allowance while eligible parents and ancestors resume [#147480](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147480).
- Let embedded system-expert requests answer and release their waiting parent when ordinary agent concurrency is one; command planning and caretaker greetings share the repair [#147596](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147596). Thanks @obviyus, @ekinnee, and @maulu5.

</details>
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Interrupted conversations and late replies">

After a restart, eligible unfinished conversations can continue from saved tool work without repeating your original message or discarding its results. Eligible tasks can also keep working when their tools have finished but the model times out. Messages arriving too late to join the current answer get their own follow-up reply. ACP background tasks that finish before cancellation takes effect keep their completed result and end time.

When prompted, recover interrupted work before sending new input, and check uncertain tool outcomes before repeating an action. New messages can wait as follow-ups while recovery handles the earlier task.

Recovery from an overfull conversation now leaves archived turns and reset markers in their original history instead of copying them into new entries. This prevents new duplication; it does not remove duplicates already stored.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Let recovery clients opt into resets bound to the session they inspected, using `expectedSessionId`; reread the session before retrying a rejected reset [#123864](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/123864). Thanks @pollybot13 and @patrick-erichsen.

**Bug fixes**

- Release later session turns when an agent run stalls [#123055](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/123055). Thanks @romneyda.
- Reply to late-arriving messages as fresh follow-up turns [#129001](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/129001). Thanks @finn763, @obviyus, and @andersonjeccel.
- Deliver genuine child replies that match the empty-output placeholder [#130647](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/130647). Thanks @zeroaltitude and @obviyus.
- Reject outdated session deletions before extra cleanup work [#144525](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144525). Thanks @hermanzeng.
- Let intentional provider retry waits finish before stale-run recovery [#144543](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144543). Thanks @obviyus and @corvid-reads.
- Recover saved task progress after Responses disconnects during a tool call [#144583](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144583).
- Keep subagent results and media on the selected conversation [#144704](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144704).
- Preserve finished background-task results during late cancellation [#144949](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144949). Thanks @gabrielnkl and @obviyus.
- Resume eligible interrupted turns after saved tool activity [#146487](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146487).
- Show restart-recovery notices in eligible conversations and typing in supported Telegram chats, respecting typing opt-outs [#141797](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/141797). Thanks @vacinc.
- Honor unusually long backoff waits in the shared retry scheduler instead of retrying immediately [#145718](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145718). Thanks @alix-007 and @altaywtf.
- Save session cancellation before acknowledging it, recover eligible interrupted sessions with their history, and show failed recovery scans in status and Doctor [#146792](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146792). Thanks @desksk.
- Preserve cross-agent `sessions_send` replies after configuration reloads [#146799](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146799). Thanks @zachisfine, @vincentkoc, and @davidcittadini.
- Finish saved-history repairs before chat recovery and cancellation release session resources [#146842](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146842).
- Prevent archived-history duplication during context-overflow recovery while keeping existing history readable [#147202](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147202). Thanks @vacinc.
- Continue eligible embedded tasks with tools after a model idle timeout, using saved results without resubmitting the original request; cancellation, pending approvals, unfinished work, and retry limits still apply [#147203](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147203). Thanks @vacinc.
- Resume eligible interrupted work before handling new messages after restart, preserving the earlier task if recovery cannot yet start [#147486](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147486).
- Show pending first chats as loading during connection recovery and display recovery notices once beneath the message box [#147362](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147362).
- Preserve the current task and accumulated tool results when embedded conversation replay removes old silent messages [#147531](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147531). Thanks @obviyus, @masterswords1, @fray-ai, and @mertbasar0.
- Recover the known session first without reading unrelated histories, searching the wider inventory when the recovery identity has moved [#148032](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/148032). Thanks @vincentkoc.
- Keep stopped hosted turns cancelled when workers exit without a final result, retain separate shutdown diagnostics, and release worker capacity only after cleanup and reconciliation finish, including journal retries. Deployed worker failures produce concise, redacted errors [#148181](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/148181).

</details>
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Hosted tool calls and Tool Search">

Tools running inside the Gateway now call it directly, removing redundant connections that could fail during parallel work. Permissions, approvals, cancellation, and message duplicate protection stay in place.

Tool Search code mode also works when OpenClaw runs under Bun, provided a compatible Node with `--permission` support is already installed. OpenClaw finds it automatically; without one, Tool Search keeps its structured-tool fallback.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Process large language-server replies with less copying [#145703](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145703).
- Reduce broad Tool Search ranking work in large catalogs [#145725](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145725).
- Reduce duplicate processing of large network tool results [#145818](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145818).
- Prepare large Code Mode catalogs faster [#146083](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146083).
- Reduce ACP metadata escaping work [#146156](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146156).
- Reduce cleanup work for verbose command output [#146406](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146406).
- Reduce repeated budget-fitting scans for large Code Mode results [#146584](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146584).

**Bug fixes**

- Keep complete streamed tool arguments when completion snapshots disagree [#139534](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/139534). Thanks @sunnyshu0925, @reisball, @ylcn91, @patrick-erichsen, and @vacinc.
- Restore Tool Search code mode under Bun [#145688](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145688).
- Avoid extra local connections when hosted agents inspect configuration [#146226](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146226).
- Avoid redundant Gateway connections in hosted tools and session cleanup [#146369](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146369). Thanks @jalfaro2876 and @luissalve.
- Keep one-shot agents alive while polling a background command so completed results are not lost to exit code 13 [#144268](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144268). Thanks @kimiyu-186 and @osolmaz.

</details>
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Busy Gateway responsiveness and diagnostics">

The Gateway can handle network traffic between queued session writes instead of waiting for the entire backlog. When a connection stalls during setup, the error now identifies the target and suggests a status check, giving you a clearer place to start troubleshooting. A full request queue shows a plain busy message; wait briefly, then retry your action.

Plugin authors using worker pools should handle overload errors from the new queue limits. Rejected work is not retried automatically; check that the original request is still authorized and within its deadline before retrying.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Reduce repeated path checks during Gateway state writes [#145582](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145582).
- Reduce completed-run tracking overhead [#145988](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145988).
- Avoid repeated HTTP client module lookups [#146133](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146133).
- Explain when the Gateway request queue is full and ask users to retry shortly [#146872](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146872).

**Bug fixes**

- Preserve Unix termination signals through startup wrappers [#144838](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144838). Thanks @shakkernerd.
- Preserve dashboard HTTP errors when repair details cannot be read [#145227](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145227).
- Release ended attempts after stuck delivery waits [#146034](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146034).
- Release Windows state handles after shutdown [#146160](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146160).
- Let Gateway network work proceed between queued session writes [#146491](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146491).
- Recognize stalled Gateway handshakes as transport timeouts [#146508](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146508).
- Coordinate diagnostic writes with session cleanup and retain failed batches [#146563](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146563).
- Reduce trajectory history cleanup pauses for large event payloads [#146639](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146639).
- Bound task-list scans when registry records disappear during reload [#146661](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146661).
- Batch inexpensive turn preparation to reduce waits before reaching the model on a busy Gateway [#147231](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147231).
- Give Gateway connections falling behind a best-effort disconnect explanation using the existing one-second grace, with bounded cleanup that protects replacement sockets; stalled peers can still disconnect without receiving the reason [#147707](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147707). Thanks @sunnyshu0925, @vincentkoc, and @aniruddhaadak80.

**Limits and compatibility**

- Share compute capacity and limit worker queues to 128 outstanding tasks and 256 MiB of reported inputs by default, with per-pool overrides; unreported input sizes count as zero, so these limits do not cap total memory [#146067](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146067).

</details>
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Bun worker connections and plugin setup">

Bundled Canvas and Voice Call configuration updates work again on the affected Bun setup path. Bun workers also keep their Gateway connection options and handle failed output without triggering an extra error. Bun also gets fixes for sessions, provider connections, and streamed Discord requests. Some operations still require Node.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Fix Bun worker connections and failed output [#146008](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146008).
- Restore bundled setup migrations on Bun [#146060](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146060).
- Repair SQLite session callbacks, protected HTTPS, and provider WebSockets under Bun; bundled channel schemas beyond 256 levels raise an error, while external schemas remain available to their validator [#147085](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147085).
- Fix Bun Discord streamed forwarding, Nostr profile URL safety checks, and HTTP rejection listener cleanup [#147360](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147360).
- Restore Codex session-history reads in Bun source runs, including aliased Bun executables, when the prepared JavaScript history worker is available [#147408](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147408).
- Restore Bun browser relay/CDP, Slack proxy, Reef, and Codex networking, share the WebSocket adapter with Mattermost, and fix Nextcloud Talk stopping during startup. Qualifying managed Codex JavaScript launches use Bun without Node on the child PATH; plugin authors gain the typed public `openclaw/plugin-sdk/websocket-runtime` adapter [#147421](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147421).

**Limits and compatibility**

- Repair Bun tooling and Gateway and desktop WebSocket connections while retaining Node for Node-owned child operations; stock Bun 1.4.2 lacks a required SQLite fix [#146807](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146807).

</details>
</Accordion>

</AccordionGroup>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Consolidate internal ownership and helpers">

Shared helpers consolidate repeated implementation, including preparation and resource handoff for image, music, and video generation.

Trajectory trace writes use fewer database calls while preserving event order and all-or-nothing flushes. A batch can temporarily hold up to 8 MiB of serialized event payloads plus overhead. Rich-payload timings are mixed, so this is not a memory-saving or universal latency improvement.

Memory Core Doctor retains an existing import limitation. A retry after partial writes can treat the import as a conflict and archive the original source, keeping its bytes. Sharing the import helper does not resolve that behavior.

<Accordion title="Keep types aligned with their contracts">

Configuration authoring types reuse existing validation schemas, leaving fewer duplicate definitions for maintainers to keep in sync. Some field descriptions have moved from generated type hovers to the source schemas.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Derive hook configuration types from validation schemas [#143985](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/143985). Thanks @romneyda.
- Derive agent-default types from a shared validation schema [#143986](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/143986). Thanks @romneyda.
- Derive MCP server configuration types from their validator [#143987](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/143987). Thanks @romneyda.
- Derive memory-search types from validation schemas [#143988](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/143988). Thanks @romneyda.
- Derive generated protocol registration from canonical schemas [#144112](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144112).

- Share Policy validation while preserving diagnostics [#144186](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144186).
- Derive cron schedule and state types from shared contracts [#144189](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144189).
- Derive message configuration types from validation schemas [#144462](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144462). Thanks @romneyda.

- Derive Gateway probe summary types from the extractor [#144555](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144555).
- Normalize Codex quota responses once for existing usage displays [#144620](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144620). Thanks @jesse-merhi.
- Derive native device setting types from their validation schemas [#144705](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144705). Thanks @jesse-merhi.

<Accordion title="Keep CI plans within their resource limits">

[Precisely scoped test changes](/ci/scope-and-routing/node-test-lanes) can keep their required execution settings without scheduling unrelated test groups. Measurements from those selected tests stay separate from full-suite timing history.

The Control UI startup JavaScript cap is now 354 KiB, with a 362,012-byte baseline. This adjusts the validation budget without shrinking browser assets.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Skip redundant destination hashes before mandatory cache restores [#144242](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144242).
- Reduce repeated scanning in coercion declaration checks [#144253](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144253).
- Give full CLI CI tests additional runner capacity [#144257](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144257).
- Avoid duplicate SDK-checker source traversal [#144298](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144298).
- Avoid unrelated tests for guarded max-lines baseline maintenance [#144359](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144359).
- Reuse verified Git tree entries during frozen-source checks [#144421](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144421).
- Preserve tooling test requirements in narrower CI plans [#144621](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144621).
- Avoid duplicate startup tests on full main CI runs [#145078](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145078).
- Overlap SDK declaration builds when capacity permits [#145090](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145090).
- Retain Android CI test timing reports [#145131](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145131).
- Reserve growth room in test typecheck partitions [#145461](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145461).
- Use smaller CI runners for eligible current artifact builds [#145611](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145611).
- Balance runtime tests across CI runners [#145956](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145956).
- Reuse idle Vitest transform caches [#146167](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146167).
- Regrouped Gateway server tests to preserve typecheck capacity [#146212](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146212).
- Reduce peak memory use during full-tree source validation [#146266](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146266).
- Reuse compiled test workers across eligible CI groups [#146353](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146353).
- Preserve precise CI test selection and separate subset timing history [#146366](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146366).
- Avoid duplicate startup corpus execution in qualifying broad pull requests [#146465](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146465).
- Share extension boundary-check inventory with one aggregate timeout and output budget [#146771](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146771).

<Accordion title="QA diagnostics and reporting">

When capability definitions change, QA reports keep historical results separate from current coverage. Upgrade the producer, aggregator, and renderer together, regenerate current evidence, and keep older artifacts for reference.

Dedicated QA commands reject explicitly blank `--scenario` values before running; omit the option to use defaults. Build and verification scripts also reject extra empty arguments before starting work.

Offline QA hosts using an outdated Crabbox executable need a supported executable or managed copy before upgrading. See the [remote testing guide](/reference/test/remote-proof).

Maintainers can compare clean built revisions with six opt-in Code Mode workloads. These tests require explicit OpenAI model selection and an API key and make paid calls. Task effects and follow-up explanations are scored separately; keep transcripts local unless publication is authorized.

Optional worker CPU and allocation profiles add overhead and can miss activity. Each worker has its own capture window, so those measurements cannot be added together as process CPU. SDK comparisons skip rendering when both references resolve to the same commit; comparisons between distinct revisions can still exhaust renderer memory.

For optional live ClawHub validation, explicitly select a hosted package with `OPENCLAW_PLUGINS_E2E_CLAWHUB_SPEC`. Without an override, the script still defaults to the synthetic local test package. The separate test-expectation correction does not resolve that default.

<Accordion title="Maintain release records and qualification inputs">

Release maintenance covers package alignment, validation tooling, and historical records. Normal extended-stable publishing is now limited to the month immediately preceding the version on main.

Fresh Full Release Validation requests must state their purpose, and publication requests must identify the packages and publication route. Use pinned tooling that supports both source and registry checks. Existing requests retain their original evidence for recovery. These checks do not authorize publication.

<Accordion title="Keep development builds compatible">

Bun can complete the existing [Control UI build path](/web/control-ui/development), and configured development connections avoid misleading refresh prompts. macOS worker packaging also preserves executable permissions so the package integrity check can pass.

Linux Crabbox setup keeps pnpm dependencies inside the workspace. Running setup again replaces only the two precisely recognized older dependency-link layouts, preserving external targets and unrelated links. Native Windows retains its existing junction layout.

**Improvements**

- Package read-only SQLite snapshot workers as standalone executables [#144448](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144448).
- Refresh dependencies and preserve validation compatibility [#145124](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145124).
- Reuse static plugin asset discovery during postbuild [#145250](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145250).
- Stage FreeBSD native runtime dependencies for update handoff as a prerequisite to full update support [#147324](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147324). Thanks @vincentkoc, @lucarinaorg.
- Run SQLite capability probes in a worker while preserving startup checks [#147866](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147866). Thanks @vincentkoc.

**Bug fixes**

- Honor the requested supported Node version when packaging private Mac app workers [#146556](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146556).
- Fix Bun UI builds and configured development refresh prompts [#144345](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144345). Thanks @vincentkoc.
- Preserve executable permissions in macOS worker packages [#145042](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145042).
- Initialize Bun SQLite early in test workers [#145198](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145198).
- Keep Linux Crabbox dependencies in the workspace and migrate precisely recognized old links [#147864](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147864).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Clarify contributor and maintenance guidance">

Contributor guidance puts the problem and practical impact first in PR descriptions, keeping risks, required actions, and useful validation easy to find. For visible UI changes, before-and-after screenshots must render in both the originating chat and the PR unless the user explicitly waives a destination.

PR cleanup stops when worktree ownership is ambiguous, orphaned, or incomplete, preserving paths and metadata for diagnosis. PR tooling also disables automatic maintenance during fetches to avoid unrelated metadata pruning.
